# sm120_blockscaled_sparse_mma_tma.hpp — Code Analysis / 代码分析
**Source / 源文件**: `include/cutlass/gemm/collective/sm120_blockscaled_sparse_mma_tma.hpp`
**Purpose / 用途**: Implements the SM120 collective GEMM header for block-scaled data handling, structured sparsity support, MMA-based mainloop structure, and TMA-based global-to-shared transfers. / 实现 SM120 的 collective GEMM 头文件，覆盖块缩放数据处理、结构化稀疏支持、基于 MMA 的主循环结构以及 基于 TMA 的全局到共享内存搬运。

## Key Concepts / 关键概念
- Template-driven collective GEMM composition / 模板驱动的 collective GEMM 组合
- Compile-time validation through aliases and `static_assert` checks / 通过别名与 `static_assert` 进行编译期校验
- Header-only specialization for CUTLASS mainloop building blocks / 面向 CUTLASS 主循环构件的头文件特化实现
- SM120 specialization / SM120 架构特化
- Block-scaled metadata, scale tensors, and promotion rules / 块缩放元数据、缩放张量与提升规则
- Sparse metadata movement and sparse MMA dispatch / 稀疏元数据搬运与稀疏 MMA 调度
